ORDER_RETURN_ACTIVITY
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to a seller when a return lifecycle activity occurs.
**OAuth Scope**  
In addition to the standard OAuth scope (<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope>), this topic requires an access token created using the Authorization Code Grant flow with the following scope:  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.return.read>  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.return>  

See [Working with OAuth scopes](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/guides-v2/authorization#working-with-oauth-scopes "Follow link") for more information.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the even that triggered the notifications was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[OrderReturnActivityData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.orderreturnactivitydata)
This container consists of specific information related to the order return activity notifications.
  * **OrderReturnActivityData**
This type defines the payload for the ORDER_RETURN_ACTIVITY notification.
**activityType**[OrderReturnActivityType](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.orderreturnactivitytype)
The enumeration value returned in this field indicates the specific order return activity that occurred.
**activityDate** string
This string is the date when the return activity occurred. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
**orderReturn**[OrderReturn](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.orderreturn)
This object contains detailed information about the return request where activity occurred.
  * **OrderReturnActivityType**
This enumeration type contains the list of order return activity events.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **RETURN_REQUESTED**  | This value indicates a return request was filed against an order line item.  |  
| **RETURN_FULFILLMENT_INITIATED**  | This value indicates that the buyer has initiated the return fulfillment process.  |  
| **RETURN_FULFILLMENT_COMPLETED**  | This value indicates the return fulfillment process was completed.  |  
| **RETURN_CLOSED**  | This value indicates the return was closed.  |  
| **RETURN_REFERRAL_CREATED**  | This value indicates that the return has been referred to eBay Customer Support.  |  
| **SELLER_RESPONSE_DUE_REMINDER**  | This value indicates that the seller must take an action against the return request. The **sellerAvailableActions** container in the payload may be referenced to see what action(s) are required and by what date.  |  
| **RETURN_REFERRAL_DECIDED**  | This value indicates that a referral decision has been reached by eBay Customer Support.  |  
| **RETURN_REFERRAL_ON_HOLD**  | This value indicates the referral is on hold so further details can be gathered before a decision outcome can be reached.  |  
  * **OrderReturn**
This type contains detailed information about the return request where activity occurred.
**returnId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the return.
**returnStatus**[ReturnStatus](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returnstatus)
This value indicates the current status of the return request.
**requester**[ReturnRequester](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returnrequester)
This value indicates the party who initiated the return request, such as the BUYER.
**requestedDate** string
This string is the date when the return was requested. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
**closedDate** string
This string is the date when the return was closed. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp. This date is only applicable and returned for closed return requests.
**seller**[User](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.user)
This object identifies the seller involved in the return request.
**sellerAvailableActions**[SellerAvailableActions](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.selleravailableactions)
This object describes the actions currently available to the seller in response to the return request.
**returnFulfillment**[ReturnFulfillment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returnfulfillment)
This object is used to indicate the current status of the item being returned by the buyer to the seller.
**returnRefund**[ReturnRefund](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returnrefund)
This object is used to indicate the current status of a refund that is due to the buyer for the returned item.
**returnCase**[ReturnCase](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returncase)
This object is only returned if the return request has been referred to eBay customer support and indicates the current status of the referral.
**returnLineItems** array of [ReturnLineItem](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_return_activity.returnlineitem)
This object identifies the line item being returned and the order to which it belongs.
  * **ReturnStatus**
This enumeration type contains the list of possible statuses for a return request.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **REQUESTED**  | This value indicates a return request was initiated against an order line item.  |  
| **OPEN**  | This value indicates the return is active and still in progress.  |  
| **CLOSED**  | This value indicates that the return is complete.  |  
| **DECLINED**  | This value indicates that the return request was declined by the seller.  |  
| **CANCELLED**  | This value indicates the return was cancelled, such as by the buyer or eBay.  |

ORDER_INQUIRY_ACTIVITY
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to a seller when when an inquiry lifecycle activity occurs.
**OAuth Scope**  
In addition to the standard OAuth scope (<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope>), this topic requires an access token created using the Authorization Code Grant flow with the following scope:  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.inquiry>[](https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.return.read)  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.inquiry.read>  

See [Working with OAuth scopes](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/guides-v2/authorization#working-with-oauth-scopes "Follow link") for more information.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the even that triggered the notifications was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[OrderInquiryActivityData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.orderinquiryactivitydata)
This container consists of specific information for the order inquiry activity notification.
  * **OrderInquiryActivityData**
This type defines the base payload for the ORDER_INQUIRY_ACTIVITY notification.
**activityType**[OrderInquiryActivityType](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.orderinquiryactivitytype)Always
The value returned in this field indicates the type of activity that occurred with the order inquiry.
**activityDate** stringAlways
This string is the date when the order inquiry activity occurred. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
**orderInquiry**[OrderInquiry](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.orderinquiry)Always
This object contains detailed information on the order inquiry associated with the activity.
  * **OrderInquiryActivityType**
This enumeration type contains the list of order inquiry activity events.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **INQUIRY_CREATED**  | This value indicates the order inquiry was opened/created.  |  
| **INQUIRY_REFERRAL_CREATED**  | This value indicates that the inquiry has been referred to eBay Customer Support.  |  
| **INQUIRY_REFERRAL_DECIDED**  | This value indicates that a decision has been made on the order inquiry that was referred to eBay customer support.  |  
| **INQUIRY_REFERRAL_ON_HOLD**  | This value indicates that the order inquiry referral is on hold so that eBay customer support can gather further details before a decision outcome is reached.  |  
| **INQUIRY_CLOSED**  | This value indicates that the order inquiry was closed.  |  
| **SELLER_RESPONSE_REMINDER**  | This value indicates that the notification event is a reminder to the seller that an action is required on their part for the order inquiry.  |  
  * **OrderInquiry**
This type contains detailed information on the order inquiry associated with the activity.
**type**[InquiryType](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.inquirytype)Always
The value returned in this field indicates the type of order inquiry associated with the event.
**inquiryId** stringAlways
This string is the unique identifier of the order inquiry.
**inquiryStatus**[InquiryStatus](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.inquirystatus)Always
The value in this field represents the status of the order inquiry.
**initiator**[Initiator](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.initiator)Always
The value returned in this field indicates the party who filed the inquiry, such as BUYER.
**openedDate** stringConditional
This string is the date when the order inquiry was opened. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
**closedDate** stringConditional
This string is the date when the order inquiry was closed. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp. This date is only applicable and returned for closed order inquiries.
**seller**[Seller](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.seller)Always
This object identifies the seller involved in the order inquiry.
**sellerAvailableActions**[SellerAvailableActions](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.selleravailableactions)Conditional
This object describes the actions currently available to the seller in response to the order inquiry.
**inquiryCase**[InquiryCase](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.inquirycase)Conditional
This object is only returned if the order inquiry has been referred to eBay customer support, and indicates the current status of the referral.
**inquiryRefund**[InquiryRefund](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.inquiryrefund)Conditional
This object is used to indicate the current status of a refund that is due to the buyer for the order inquiry.
**inquiryLineItems** array of [InquiryLineItem](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-order-management-order_inquiry_activity.inquirylineitem)Always
This object identifies the line item and the order that is associated with the order inquiry.
  * **InquiryType**
This enumeration type defines the different types of order inquiries.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **ITEM_NOT_RECEIVED**  | This value indicates that an Item Not Received (INR) inquiry has been filed against the line item. Buyers may open INR inquiries if they have not received their item within the expected delivery range and/or the seller has not provided any shipment tracking information.  |

##### Offers and Leads
Offers and Leads-related notifications applicable for buyers and sellers
OFFER_ACTIVITY
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
Notifications sent to buyer or seller when listing offer activity occurs.
A subscription for this topic initiates offer-related notifications being sent to a buyer or seller when listing offer activity occurs.
The four notifications that may be sent are:
  * BUYER_OFFER (seller only)
  * BUYER_COUNTER_OFFER (seller only)
  * SELLER_OFFER (buyer only)
  * SELLER_COUNTER_OFFER (buyer only).


###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **Metadata**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[Payload](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-offers-and-leads-offer_activity.payload)
This container consists of specific information related to the payload for the offer events.
  * **Payload**
This type is the payload for best offer events.
**offerId** string
This string is the unique ID for the offer record.
**itemId** string
This string indicates the item on which the offer activity took place.
**status**[OfferStatus](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-offers-and-leads-offer_activity.offerstatus)
This object represents the current state of the offer in its lifecycle
**offerType**[OfferType](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-offers-and-leads-offer_activity.offertype)
This object references the type of the offer based on the current negotiation status. It indicates if the offer was sent by the seller or the buyer and if this was an initial or counter offer.
**seller**[User](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-offers-and-leads-offer_activity.user)
This object represents the seller.
**buyer**[User](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-offers-and-leads-offer_activity.user)
This object represents the buyer who placed the offer.
  * **OfferStatus**
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field to indicate the current status of the offer.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **PENDING**  | The offer is awaiting a response from the buyer.  |  
| **DECLINED**  | The buyer has declined the seller's offer.  |  
| **ACCEPTED**  | The buyer has accepted the offer that was sent by the seller.  |  
| **EXPIRED**  | The offer reached its deadline and is no longer available to the buyer.  |  
  * **OfferType**
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field to describe the offer based on the current negotiation status. It also indicate who sent this offer and whether this was an initial offer or a counter offer.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **BUYER_OFFER**  | This is the offer initiated by the buyer on a listing, pending seller response.  |  
| **BUYER_COUNTER_OFFER**  | This is a buyer’s revised offer in response to a previous seller offer..  |  
| **SELLER_OFFER**  | This is a seller-initiated offer to sell the item to the buyer at a specified price.  |  
| **SELLER_COUNTER_OFFER**  | This is a seller's revised offer in response to a previous buyer offer.  |

##### Communication
Communication-related notifications applicable for buyers and sellers
BUYER_QUESTION
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to a seller when a buyer asks a question about the seller’s listing.
Use this event to receive a notification when a potential buyer/bidder asks a question on an active listing.
**OAuth Scope**  
In addition to the standard OAuth scope (<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope>), this topic requires an access token created using the Authorization Code Grant flow with the following scope:  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/commerce.message>  

See [Working with OAuth scopes](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/guides-v2/authorization#working-with-oauth-scopes "Follow link") for more information.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[BuyerQuestionData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-buyer_question.buyerquestiondata)
This container consists of specific information related to the buyer question data.
  * **BuyerQuestionData**
This type defines the payload for the BUYER_QUESTION notification.
**messageId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the message.
**conversationId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the conversation.
**messageBody** string
This string is the body text of the message.
**listingId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the listing associated with the conversation.
**senderUserName** string
This string is the eBay username of the sender.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html). 
**recipientUserName** string
This string is the eBay username of the recipient.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html). 
**subject** string
This string is the subject of the message.
**readStatus** boolean
This Boolean indicates if the message has been read.
**createdDate** string
This timestamp indicates when the message was created, in ISO 8601 format.
**messageMedia** array of object
This array is a list of media attachments associated with a message.

FEEDBACK_LEFT
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to a buyer or seller when their feedback for their order partner has been recorded by eBay.
This notification is sent to a seller or buyer when the feedback they have left for their order partner for a line item has been recorded.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
ECC message signature


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[FeedbackData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.feedbackdata)
This container consists of specific information related to the feedback data.
  * **FeedbackData**
This type defines the payload for the Feedback Left notification.
**receiverUserDetail**[ReceiverUserDetail](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.receiveruserdetail)
This object contains the information about the user who received the feedback.
**feedbackDetail**[FeedbackDetail](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.feedbackdetail)
This type includes the details of the feedback left by the user.
**commentType** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the type of feedback being received. When leaving feedback, only a buyer can use these ratings for a seller. A seller can only leave a buyer Positive feedback.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **POSITIVE**  | This value indicates that the feedback expresses satisfaction with the transaction or experience. This typically reflects a successful and favorable interaction between the buyer and seller.  |  
| **NEUTRAL**  | This value indicates that the feedback is neither distinctly favorable nor unfavorable. The transaction was completed, but did not fully meet expectations, or was considered average.  |  
| **NEGATIVE**  | This value indicates dissatisfaction with the transaction or experience. This type of feedback highlights issues or problems that occurred, signaling that the buyer or seller was not satisfied.  |  
**feedbackComment**[FeedbackComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.feedbackcomment)
This object contains the feedback comment left by the user.
**replyComment**[ReplyComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.replycomment)
This object contains the feedback reply comment.
**followupComment**[FollowupComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.followupcomment)
This object contains the feedback follow-up comment.
**providerUserDetail**[ProviderUserDetail](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_left.provideruserdetail)
This object contains information about the user who provided the feedback.
**feedbackState** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the state of the entire feedback as a whole.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **ENTERED**  | This value indicates that the feedback has been submitted and is currently active. The feedback is visible to applicable parties.  |  
| **DESCORED**  | This value indicates that the feedback has a score impact of 0 and does not contribute to the overall feedback score.  |  
| **MUTUAL_WITHDRAWAL_INITIATED**  | This value indicates that the buyer or seller has started a mutual feedback withdrawal process and agreement from the other party is pending.  |  
| **FEEDBACK_REVISION_OPEN**  | This value indicates that a feedback revision request has been sent and is currently open and awaiting a response.  |  
| **FEEDBACK_REVISION_CLOSED**  | This value indicates tha tthe feedback revision request has been closed either because the request was revised, declined, or it expired.  |  
**feedbackEnteredDate** string
This string is the date when the feedback was entered. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
  * **ReceiverUserDetail**
This object contains the information about the user who received the feedback.
**userId** string
This string is the unique, public identifier for the user who received the feedback.
**role** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the role of the user who provided the feedback.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **BUYER**  | This value indicates that the receiver of the feedback is a buyer.  |  
| **SELLER**  | This value indicates that the receiver of the feedback is a seller.  |  
  * **ListingPrice**
The price information of the item for which feedback has been left.
**currency** string
The currency used for the price of the item..
**value** number
The price of the item.

Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field to indicate the type of feedback being received. When leaving feedback, only a buyer can use these ratings for a seller. A seller can only leave a buyer Positive feedback.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **POSITIVE**  | This value indicates that the feedback expresses satisfaction with the transaction or experience. This typically reflects a successful and favorable interaction between the buyer and seller.  |  
| **NEUTRAL**  | This value indicates that the feedback is neither distinctly favorable nor unfavorable. The transaction was completed, but did not fully meet expectations, or was considered average.  |  
| **NEGATIVE**  | This value indicates dissatisfaction with the transaction or experience. This type of feedback highlights issues or problems that occurred, signaling that the buyer or seller was not satisfied.  |  
**feedbackComment**[FeedbackComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_received.feedbackcomment)
This object contains the feedback comment received by the user.
**replyComment**[ReplyComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_received.replycomment)
This object contains the feedback reply comment.
**followupComment**[FollowupComment](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_received.followupcomment)
This object contains the feedback follow up comment.
**providerUserDetail**[ProviderUserDetail](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-feedback_received.provideruserdetail)
This object contains information about the user who provided the feedback.
**feedbackState** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field as the possible state of the entire feedback as a whole.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **ENTERED**  | This value indicates that the feedback has been submitted and is currently active. The feedback is visible to applicable parties.  |  
| **DESCORED**  | This value indicates that the feedback has a score impact of 0 and does not contribute to the overall feedback score.  |  
| **MUTUAL_WITHDRAWAL_INITIATED**  | This value indicates that the buyer or seller has started a mutual feedback withdrawal process and agreement from the other party is pending.  |  
| **FEEDBACK_REVISION_OPEN**  | This value indicates that a feedback revision request has been sent and is currently open and awaiting a response.  |  
| **FEEDBACK_REVISION_CLOSED**  | This value indicates tha tthe feedback revision request has been closed either because the request was revised, declined, or it expired.  |  
**feedbackEnteredDate** string
This string is the date when the feedback was entered. This date is returned as a UTC-formatted timestamp.
  * **ReceiverUserDetail**
This object contains the information about the user who received the feedback.
**userId** string
This string is the unique, public identifier for the user who received the feedback.
**role** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field as the role of the user who provided the feedback.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **BUYER**  | This value indicates that the receiver of the feedback is a buyer.  |  
| **SELLER**  | This value indicates that the receiver of the feedback is a seller.  |  
  * **ListingPrice**
This object contains the price information of the item for which feedback has been received.
**currency** string
This string indicates the currency used for the price of the item.
**value** number
The price of the item.

NEW_MESSAGE
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
Notification when a user receives a new message.
Sellers or buyers subscribed to this topic will get a notification each time another member or eBay sends them a new message that starts or continues a conversation.
**OAuth Scope**  
In addition to the standard OAuth scope (<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope>), this topic requires an access token created using the Authorization Code Grant flow with the following scope:  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/commerce.message>  

See [Working with OAuth scopes](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/guides-v2/authorization#working-with-oauth-scopes "Follow link") for more information.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[NewMessageData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-new_message.newmessagedata)
This container consists of specific information related to the new message data.
  * **NewMessageData**
This type defines the payload for the NEW_MESSAGE notification.
**messageId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the message.
**conversationType** string
The enum value in this field indicates if the new message is from another eBay user or from eBay.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **FROM_MEMBERS**  | This value indicates the message is being sent by an eBay member.  |  
| **FROM_EBAY**  | This value indicates the message is being sent from eBay.  |  
**conversationId** string
This string is the unique identifier of the conversation.
**messageBody** string
This string is the body text of the message.
**senderUserName** string
This string represents the eBay username of the sender.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html). 
**recipientUserName** string
This string represents the eBay username of the recipient.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html). 
**subject** string
This string is the subject of the message.
**readStatus** boolean
This Boolean Indicates if the message has been read.
**createdDate** string
This timestamp indicates when the message was created, in ISO 8601 format.
**messageMedia** array of object
This array is a list of media attachments associated with a message.

REGULATORY_GUIDELINE
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
Sends a notification when eBay publishes a new or updated regulatory guideline that requires seller attention or action.
Subscribing to this topic triggers automated notifications to developers when eBay publishes a new or updated regulatory guideline that requires seller attention or action.
The notification payload contains seller-facing content, such as title, description, required actions, targeting metadata to identify the relevant seller audience, and optional technical details about related eBay API changes.
The notification payload provides context for the event, including:
  * **Guideline content:** Localized seller-facing title, description, required action, and consequence of inaction.
  * **Applicability:** Seller account types, seller locations, affected marketplaces, and shipping destinations.
  * **User alert timing:** Recommended start and end dates for displaying the alert to sellers.
  * **Affected APIs:** Optional API impact summary and technical resources for integration teams.


**Note:** Notifications may include content in one or more BCP 47 language codes, such as `en`, `de`, or `ja`. Implement a fallback to `en` if the seller's preferred language is not present in the `guidelineContents` array.
Access to this topic is controlled and may only be available to approved partners. Use the `getTopics` method to determine the required OAuth scopes for this topic.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** stringAlways
This is the unique identifier of the notification topic. For this notification, the value is REGULATORY_GUIDELINE.
**schemaVersion** stringAlways
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** booleanAlways
This Boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** stringAlways
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** stringAlways
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time, in UTC format, when the event that triggered the notification occurred.
**publishDate** stringAlways
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time, in UTC format, when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integerAlways
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[RegulatoryGuidelineData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.regulatoryguidelinedata)Always
This container consists of specific information related to the payload for the regulatory guideline notification.
  * **RegulatoryGuidelineData**
This type is the payload for the regulatory guideline notification. The payload contains details about the regulatory guideline, including the guideline identifier, version, lifecycle status, publication date, seller-facing content, applicability criteria, recommended alert timing, and any related eBay API impact details.
**regulationGuidelineId** stringAlways
This string value is the stable identifier of the regulatory guideline. The same regulationGuidelineId can be returned across multiple notifications when the guideline is updated. Use this value with version to identify and process updates to the same guideline.
**version** stringAlways
This string value is the version number of the regulatory guideline. A higher version value indicates a more recent version of the same regulatory guideline.
**status** stringAlways
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the lifecycle status of the regulatory guideline.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **ACTIVE**  | This indicates that the regulatory guideline is currently active.  |  
**publishedDate** stringAlways
This timestamp indicates the date and time, in UTC format, when eBay published this version of the regulatory guideline.
**versionChangeSummary** stringConditional
This string value provides a summary of the changes made in this version of the regulatory guideline.
**regulationGuideline**[RegulatoryGuideline](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.regulatoryguideline)Conditional
This container consists of information about the regulatory guideline, including the guideline type, reference URL, effective date, and expiration date.
**applicability**[Applicability](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.applicability)Conditional
This container consists of the criteria that determine which sellers, marketplaces, and shipping destinations are affected by the regulatory guideline.
**userAlert**[UserAlert](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.useralert)Conditional
This container consists of the recommended start and end dates for displaying the regulatory guideline notification to sellers.
**affectedApis**[AffectedApis](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.affectedapis)Conditional
This container consists of information about eBay public APIs that may be affected by the regulatory guideline.
**guidelineContents** array of [GuidelineContent](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-communication-%20regulatory_guideline.guidelinecontent)Conditional
This array contains the seller-facing content for the regulatory guideline. Each object in the array contains content for a specific language. If the seller preferred language is not available, use the English content when it is provided.
  * **RegulatoryGuideline**
This type defines the regulatory guideline details.
**type** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the type of regulatory guideline.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **REGULATORY_GUIDELINE**  | This indicates that the notification is for a regulatory guideline that may require seller attention or action.  |  
| **EBAY_INTERNAL_TEST**  | This indicates that the guideline is an eBay internal test entry. Internal test entries are used for integration testing only and should be filtered out in production display logic.  |  
**referenceUrl** string
This string value is the URL to the eBay Help Center page or other eBay reference page for the regulatory guideline.
**effectiveDate** string
This timestamp indicates the date and time, in UTC format, when the regulatory guideline becomes effective or when sellers must complete the required action.
**expirationDate** string
This timestamp indicates the date and time, in UTC format, when the regulatory guideline expires or stops applying. Null may be returned if there is no expiration date.
  * **Applicability**
This type defines the applicability criteria for the regulatory guideline.
**applicableTo** array of string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the role to which the regulatory guideline applies.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **SELLER**  | This indicates that the regulatory guideline applies to sellers.  |  
**userAccountTypes** array of string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the seller account types to which the regulatory guideline applies.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **BUSINESS**  | This indicates that the regulatory guideline applies to business seller accounts.  |  
| **INDIVIDUAL**  | This indicates that the regulatory guideline applies to individual seller accounts.  |  
**userActionRequired** boolean
This Boolean value indicates whether the seller must take action to comply with the regulatory guideline.
**userLocations** array of string
This array contains the ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country codes for the seller locations to which the regulatory guideline applies.
**marketplaces** array of string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field for the eBay marketplace sites affected by the regulatory guideline.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **EBAY_AT**  | This indicates the eBay Austria marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_AU**  | This indicates the eBay Australia marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_BEFR**  | This indicates the eBay Belgium French marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_BENL**  | This indicates the eBay Belgium Dutch marketplace.  |

| **EBAY_CAFR**  | This indicates the eBay Canada French marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_CH**  | This indicates the eBay Switzerland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_DE**  | This indicates the eBay Germany marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_ES**  | This indicates the eBay Spain marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_FR**  | This indicates the eBay France marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_GB**  | This indicates the eBay United Kingdom marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_HK**  | This indicates the eBay Hong Kong marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_IE**  | This indicates the eBay Ireland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_IT**  | This indicates the eBay Italy marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_MOTORS_US**  | This indicates the eBay Motors US marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_MY**  | This indicates the eBay Malaysia marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_NL**  | This indicates the eBay Netherlands marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_PH**  | This indicates the eBay Philippines marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_PL**  | This indicates the eBay Poland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_SG**  | This indicates the eBay Singapore marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_US**  | This indicates the eBay United States marketplace.  |  
**shippingDestinations** array of string
This array contains the ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country codes for the shipping destinations to which the regulatory guideline applies.

##### Marketing and Promotions
Promoted Listing or Item Discount notifications for sellers
PLA_CAMPAIGN_BUDGET_STATUS
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
Sent when a Promoted Listings priority strategy campaign budget is exhausted.
A subscription for this topic initiates notifications being sent to sellers when their Promoted Listings priority strategy ad campaign (formerly Promoted Listings Advanced (PLA)) daily budget has been completely exhausted.
**OAuth Scope**  
In addition to the standard OAuth scope (<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope>), this topic requires an access token created using the Authorization Code Grant flow with the following scopes:  
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.marketing>,   
<https://api.ebay.com/oauth/api_scope/sell.marketing.readonly>
See [Working with OAuth scopes](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/guides-v2/authorization#working-with-oauth-scopes "Follow link") for more information.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
The ECC message signature. This signature is used to process and validate the message payload.


##### Types
  * **Metadata**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[PLACampaignBudgetStatusData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-marketing-and-promotions-pla_campaign_budget_status.placampaignbudgetstatusdata)
This container consists of specific information related to the PLA campaign budget status data.
  * **PLACampaignBudgetStatusData**
This type defines the payload for the PLA campaign budget status notification.
**user**[User](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-marketing-and-promotions-pla_campaign_budget_status.user)
This object contains the information about the user that is the notification subscriber.
**campaignId** string
This string is the unique identifier for the PLA campaign that has an exhausted daily budget.
**budgetStatus** string
The enum value returned in this field indicates the status of the daily budget for the PLA campaign.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **OUT_OF_BUDGET**  | This value indicates that the daily budget for the campaign has been exhausted.  |  
  * **User**
This type is the user details for the notification subscriber.
**userId** string
This string is the unique identifier for the user.
**username** string
This string is the username of the seller.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html).

##### Inventory Discovery and Refresh
Buyer-facing notifications concerning inventory discovery and listing revisions
ITEM_PRICE_REVISION
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to eBay Partner Network partners when an item's price changes.
This notification is sent to an eBay Partner Network partners when a listing’s Buy it Now price and/or the minimum bid price changes for an auction listing.
**Note** : Currently, this topic is available for eBay Partner Network (ePN) partners in all marketplaces **except** the following: CA, AU, HK, SG, MY, and PH.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
ECC message signature


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[ItemPriceRevisionData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_price_revision.itempricerevisiondata)
This container consists of specific information related to the item price revision data.
  * **ItemPriceRevisionData**
This type defines the Item Price Revision payload.
**itemId** string
The unique identifier of a purchasable item.
**listingMarketplaceId** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field and indicate the standard codes that represent each of the eBay marketplaces.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **EBAY_US**  | eBay US (ebay.com)  |  
| **EBAY_GB**  | eBay UK (ebay.co.uk)  |  
| **EBAY_AU**  | eBay Australia (ebay.com/au)  |  
| **EBAY_AT**  | eBay Austria (ebay.at)  |  
| **EBAY_FR**  | eBay France (ebay.fr)  |  
| **EBAY_DE**  | eBay Germany (ebay.de)  |  
| **EBAY_IT**  | eBay Italy (ebay.it)  |  
| **EBAY_BE**  | eBay Belgium (ebay.com/sch/Belgium)  |  
| **EBAY_NL**  | eBay Netherlands (ebay.nl)  |  
| **EBAY_ES**  | eBay Spain (ebay.es)  |  
| **EBAY_CH**  | eBay Switzerland (ebay.ch)  |  
| **EBAY_HK**  | eBay Hong Kong (ebay.com.hk)  |  
| **EBAY_IE**  | eBay Ireland (ebay.ie)  |  
| **EBAY_MY**  | eBay Malaysia (ebay.com/my)  |  
| **EBAY_CA**  | eBay Canada (English) (ebay.ca)  |  
| **EBAY_PH**  | eBay Philippines (ebay.ph)  |  
| **EBAY_PL**  | eBay Poland (ebay.pl)  |  
| **EBAY_SG**  | eBay Singapore (ebay.com/sg)  |  
**seller**[Seller](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_price_revision.seller)
This object contains the information of the seller.
**price**[Price](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_price_revision.price)
This object contains the pricing information of the item.
**minimumBidPrice**[MinimumBidPrice](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_price_revision.minimumbidprice)
This object contains information on the minimum bid placed on the item.
**buyingOptions** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field that defines the buyer options for the item.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **AUCTION**  | This enumeration value indicates that the line item is available for purchase through an auction listing by being the highest bidder at auction end.  |  
| **FIXED_PRICE**  | This enumeration value indicates that the line item is available for purchase through a fixed price listing or through an auction listing with the ‘Buy it Now’ feature enabled.  |  
| **BEST_OFFER**  | This enumeration value indicates that the line item is available for purchase through the Best Offer process if Best Offer is enabled on a fixed price listing or an auction listing.  |  
**categoryId** string
The unique identifier of the primary listing category.
**metaCategoryId** string
The unique identifier of the primary listing category’s parent category.
**categoryName** string
The canonical name of the primary (leaf) category.
**categoryPath** string
The full path to the primary (leaf) category.
  * **Seller**
This object contains the information of the seller.
**username** string
This string is the username of the seller.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html).
  * **Price**
This object contains the pricing information of the item.
**currency** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field that include the three-letter [ISO 4217](https://www.iso.org/iso-4217-currency-codes.html "https://www.iso.org") code representing the supported world currencies.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **AED**  | United Arab Emirates dirham  |  
| **AFN**  | Afghan afghani  |  
| **ALL**  | Albanian lek  |  
| **AMD**  | Armenian dram  |  
| **AOA**  | Angolan kwanza  |  
| **ARS**  | Argentine peso  |  
| **AWG**  | Aruban florin  |  
| **AZN**  | Azerbaijani manat  |  
| **BAM**  | Bosnia and Herzegovina convertible mark  |  
| **BBD**  | Barbados dollar  |  
| **BDT**  | Bangladeshi taka  |  
| **BGN**  | Bulgarian lev  |  
| **BHD**  | Bahraini dinar  |  
| **BIF**  | Burundian franc  |  
| **BMD**  | Bermudian dollar  |  
| **BND**  | Brunei dollar  |

PRIORITY_LISTING_REVISION
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
This notification is sent to eBay Partner Network partners when an item has been added to or removed from a Promoted Listing Priority campaign.
A subscription for this topic initiates notifications being sent to sellers and buyers when a Priority Listing has been revised.
**Note:** Currently, this topic is available for eBay Partner Network (ePN) partners in all marketplaces **except** the following: CA, AU, HK, SG, MY, and PH.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
ECC message signature


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[PriorityListingRevisionData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-priority_listing_revision.prioritylistingrevisiondata)
This container consists of specific information related to the priority listing revision data.
  * **PriorityListingRevisionData**
This type defines the Priority Listing Revision payload.
**itemId** string
The unique identifier of the purchasable item.
**primaryItemGroupId** string
The unique identifier of the item group that the item belongs to. This field is only returned if the purchasable item is variation within a multiple-variation listing.
**listingMarketplaceId** string
Below is the list of enums that may be returned in this field that represent the standard codes for each of the eBay marketplaces.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **EBAY_US**  | eBay US (ebay.com)  |  
| **EBAY_GB**  | eBay UK (ebay.co.uk)  |  
| **EBAY_AU**  | eBay Australia (ebay.com/au)  |  
| **EBAY_AT**  | eBay Austria (ebay.at)  |  
| **EBAY_FR**  | eBay France (ebay.fr)  |  
| **EBAY_DE**  | eBay Germany (ebay.de)  |  
| **EBAY_IT**  | eBay Italy (ebay.it)  |  
| **EBAY_BE**  | eBay Belgium (ebay.com/sch/Belgium)  |  
| **EBAY_NL**  | eBay Netherlands (ebay.nl)  |  
| **EBAY_ES**  | eBay Spain (ebay.es)  |  
| **EBAY_CH**  | eBay Switzerland (ebay.ch)  |  
| **EBAY_HK**  | eBay Hong Kong (ebay.com.hk)  |  
| **EBAY_IE**  | eBay Ireland (ebay.ie)  |  
| **EBAY_MY**  | eBay Malaysia (ebay.com/my)  |  
| **EBAY_CA**  | eBay Canada (English) (ebay.ca)  |  
| **EBAY_PH**  | eBay Philippines (ebay.ph)  |  
| **EBAY_PL**  | eBay Poland (ebay.pl)  |  
| **EBAY_SG**  | eBay Singapore (ebay.com/sg)  |  
**seller**[Seller](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-priority_listing_revision.seller)
This object contains the information of the seller.
**categoryId** string
The unique identifier of the primary listing category.
**metaCategoryId** string
The unique identifier of the primary listing category’s parent category.
**priorityListing** boolean
Thos Boolean indicates if the purchasable item is a part of a Promoted Listing Priority campaign.
  * **Seller**
This object contains the information of the seller.
**userName** string
This string is the username of the seller.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html). 
**userId** string
This string is the userId of the seller.

ITEM_AVAILABILITY
Copy Markdown| View as Markdown
Sent when a fixed-price item’s availability type changes.
A subscription for this topic initiates notifications being sent to a developer when a fixed-price item's availability type changes.
Supported availability types are:
  * AVAILABLE
  * UNAVAILABLE
  * TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE


**Note:** Currently, this topic is available for eBay Partner Network (ePN) partners in all marketplaces **except** the following: CA, AU, HK, SG, MY, and PH.
###### Delivery
Method
HTTPS webhook (POST)
Retries
3
###### HTTP headers
  * **X-EBAY-SIGNATURE**
ECC message signature


##### Types
  * **MetaData**
This type specifies the metadata included with the notification.
**topic** string
This is the unique identifier of the Notification topic.
**schemaVersion** string
This specifies the version number of the topic schema.
**deprecated** boolean
This boolean field indicates whether or not the topic is active or deprecated.
  * **Notification**
This type includes information about the notification.
**notificationId** string
This is the unique identifier of the specific notification.
**eventDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the event that triggered the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishDate** string
This is the timestamp that indicates the date and time (in UTC format) when the notification was sent to the recipient.
**publishAttemptCount** integer
This integer value indicates whether this is the first, second, or third attempt to send the notification to the recipient. When a notification is sent successfully the first time, the integer value returned will be 1.
**data**[ItemAvailabilityData](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_availability.itemavailabilitydata)
This container consists of specific information related to the item availability data.
  * **ItemAvailabilityData**
This type encompasses the Item Availability payload.
**itemId** string
This is the ID for the item.
**listingMarketplaceId** string
This enumeration type is used to indicate the eBay marketplace where the item is listed.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **EBAY_US**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay US marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_GB**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay UK marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_AU**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Australia marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_AT**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Austria marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_FR**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay France marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_DE**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Germany marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_IT**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Italy marketplace  |  
| **EBAY_BE**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Belgium marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_NL**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Netherlands marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_ES**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Spain marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_CH**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Switzerland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_HK**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Hong Kong marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_IE**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Ireland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_MY**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Malaysia marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_CA**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Canada marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_PH**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Philippines marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_PL**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Poland marketplace.  |  
| **EBAY_SG**  | This value indicates that the listing is on the eBay Singapore marketplace.  |  
**buyingOptions** array
This array shows the list of the purchase options available to the buyer to purchase the item.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **FIXED_PRICE**  | This value indicates that the item can be directly purchased through a fixed price listing.  |  
| **AUCTION**  | This value indicates that the buyer can win the item through an auction listing by being the highest bidder at the end of the auction.  |  
| **BEST_OFFER**  | This value indicates that the buyer can propose a Best Offer to the seller for the item.  |  
**seller**[Seller](https://developer.ebay.com/develop/api/sell/notification_events#sell-notification_events-inventory-discovery-and-refresh-item_availability.seller)
This object includes the seller information.
**categoryId** string
The unique identifier of the eBay primary (leaf) category.
**metaCategoryId** string
The unique identifier of the parent category of the leaf category defined in the categoryId field.
**categoryName** string
The canonical name of the primary (leaf) category.
**categoryPath** string
The full path to the primary (leaf) category
**availability** string
The enum returned here indicates the current availability of the item.
**Possible Values:**  
| Value  | Description  |  
| --- | --- |  
| **TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE**  | This value indicates that the item can’t be purchased through the listing at the moment, which could be due to the seller having their account set to vacation mode or the item being out of stock.  |  
| **AVAILABLE**  | This value indicates that the item is available for purchase through the listing.  |  
| **UNAVAILABLE**  | This value indicates that the item is no longer available and isn’t expected to be available any longer under that listing ID due to listing being ended.  |  
  * **Seller**
This object includes the seller information.
**username** string
This string is the username of the seller.  
  
**Note:** Effective September 26, 2025, select developers will no longer receive username data for U.S. users through this field. Instead, an immutable user ID will be returned in its place. For more information, please refer to [Data Handling Compliance](https://developer.ebay.com/api-docs/static/data-handling-update.html).
